Kết quả chấm bài

Unique defects: 1. Timestamp variation (golden-0 vs student-0), 2. Missing hash in chunk_19, 3. Incorrect TLS port claim in chunk_16, 4. Missing evidence reference in chunk_19, 5. Disordered timeline in chunk_10, 6. Placeholder in chunk_10, 7. Unsupported causal claim in chunk_16, 8. Missing core behavior (registry persistence) in chunk_13, 9. Missing core behavior (network traffic) in chunk_14, 10. Missing core behavior (file system) in chunk_3.
